</TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>12. Subsequent Events </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">On November&nbsp;8, 2008, the Company&#146;s Board of Directors appointed John D. Goodman as the Company&#146;s Chief Executive Officer, Emilia Fabricant as our President and Chief Merchandising Officer and Frederick
G. Silny as our Executive Vice President, Chief Financial Officer, Treasurer and Corporate Secretary, each contingent and effective upon his or her commencement of employment. In addition, Mr.&nbsp;Goodman and Ms.&nbsp;Fabricant were appointed as
directors, contingent and effective upon his or her commencement of employment. Mr.&nbsp;Silny commenced employment on November&nbsp;10, 2008 and Mr.&nbsp;Goodman and Ms.&nbsp;Fabricant commenced employment on November&nbsp;12, 2008. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">F-18 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" COLOR="#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">
<h5 align="left"><a href="#toc">Table of Contents</a></h5>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The Company entered into offer letters with Mr.&nbsp;Goodman, Ms.&nbsp;Fabricant and Mr.&nbsp;Silny that
provide for annual base salaries of $900,000, $600,000 and $400,000, respectively, which may not be decreased, except for decreases in base salary consistent with decreases applicable to all of the Company&#146;s executive officers that occur after
fiscal year 2009. Mr.&nbsp;Goodman, Ms.&nbsp;Fabricant and Mr.&nbsp;Silny also received signing bonuses of $285,000, $210,000 and $160,000, respectively, which vest monthly during the 12 months following commencement of employment and are subject to
repayment of any unvested portion if the executive officer is terminated for cause or without good reason. The offer letters also provide for the executive officers to participate in the Company&#146;s Executive Officer Compensation Program for
fiscal 2009 with respect to cash incentive bonuses, which currently provides for annual cash incentive bonus targets for Mr.&nbsp;Goodman, Ms.&nbsp;Fabricant and Mr.&nbsp;Silny as follows: 50%, 100% and 150%; 50%, 75% and 100%; and 35%, 60% and
100%, respectively (as a minimum, midpoint and maximum percentage of annual base salary). Each executive officer will also be reimbursed for moving-related expenses (and eligible to receive an additional tax gross-up payment to the extent such
reimbursement is taxable), subject to a $100,000 limitation in the case of Mr.&nbsp;Silny. If any of the executive officers&#146; employment is terminated without cause or for good reason, the executive officer is entitled to (i)&nbsp;24 months (12
months in the case of Mr.&nbsp;Silny) of continued payment of his or her annual base salary in effect at the time of termination, (ii)&nbsp;a pro-rated bonus for the fiscal year in which the termination occurs equal to the bonus that would have been
paid had the executive officer remained employed through the time such bonus is paid out, multiplied by a fraction equal to the number of days elapsed since the commencement of the applicable fiscal year (as of the date of termination) divided by
360, and all the earned, but unpaid bonuses with respect to the fiscal years preceding the fiscal year of termination, (iii)&nbsp;an extension of the post termination exercise period for vested stock options to six months and (iv)&nbsp;the provision
of continued health insurance benefits for 24 months (12 months in the case of Mr.&nbsp;Silny) post termination, in each case provided certain conditions are met and subject to reduction due to interim earnings during the severance period or
cessation in the event of obtaining new employment of equal or higher compensation prior to the end of the severance period. On November&nbsp;12, 2008, Mr.&nbsp;Goodman received a non-qualified stock option to purchase 200,000 shares of the
Company&#146;s common stock and restricted stock units covering 91,000 shares of the Company&#146;s common stock. The exercise price per share of the stock option is $8.15 (the NASDAQ closing price of the Company&#146;s common stock on
November&nbsp;12, 2008). The shares subject to the stock option and restricted stock units vest in equal yearly installments over a three-year period following the date of grant and the stock option expires 10 years from the date of grant. In
addition, within 60 days of the date of commencement of his employment, Mr.&nbsp;Goodman will receive performance share units covering 53,500 shares of the Company&#146;s common stock. Contingent on the satisfaction of performance goals established
by the Compensation Committee and developed with Mr.&nbsp;Goodman&#146;s input, and with a performance period of our 2009 fiscal year, the shares subject to the performance share units will vest in equal yearly installments over a four-year period
following the date of commencement of Mr.&nbsp;Goodman&#146;s employment. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Also on November&nbsp;12, 2008, Ms.&nbsp;Fabricant received an
incentive stock option to purchase 128,500 shares of the Company&#146;s common stock and restricted stock units covering 46,000 shares of the Company&#146;s common stock. The exercise price per share of the stock option is $8.15 (the NASDAQ closing
price of the Company&#146;s common stock on November&nbsp;12, 2008). The shares subject to the stock option and restricted stock units vest in equal yearly installments over a three-year period following the date of grant and the stock option
expires 10 years from the date of grant. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">On November&nbsp;10, 2008, Mr.&nbsp;Silny received a non-qualified stock option to purchase
173,500 shares of the Company&#146;s common stock at an exercise price per share of $6.45 (the NASDAQ closing price of the Company&#146;s common stock on November&nbsp;10, 2008). The shares subject to the stock option vest in equal yearly
installments over a three-year period following the date of grant and the stock option expires 10 years from the date of grant. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The shares
subject to the awards granted, or to be granted, to Mr.&nbsp;Goodman, Ms.&nbsp;Fabricant and Mr.&nbsp;Silny will immediately vest and become fully exercisable upon a change of control, provided that, in the case of the performance share units to be
granted to Mr.&nbsp;Goodman, the applicable performance goals have been met. Commencing in fiscal 2010, Mr.&nbsp;Goodman, Ms.&nbsp;Fabricant and Mr.&nbsp;Silny will be eligible to participate in the Company&#146;s Executive Officer Compensation
Program with respect to equity awards. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Leonard H. Mogil ceased serving as the Company&#146;s Interim Chief Financial Officer concurrent
with Mr.&nbsp;Silny&#146;s commencement of employment on November&nbsp;10, 2008 and ceased serving as the Company&#146;s Interim Chief Executive Officer concurrent with Mr.&nbsp;Goodman&#146;s commencement of employment on November&nbsp;12, 2008.
Mr.&nbsp;Mogil remains a member of the Board of Directors. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
